\displaystyle\lesssim\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{U}\|\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}|\widehat{\Lambda}|^{1/2}-|\Lambda|^{1/2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{K}\theta_{i}}{\|\theta\|}\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}|\widehat{\Lambda}|^{1/2}-|\Lambda|^{1/2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{K}\theta_{i}}{\|\theta\|}\frac{K^{2}}{\lambda^{1/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{K}\theta_{i}}{\|\theta\|}\frac{K^{5/2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{K^{3}\theta_{i}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}}\bigg).

(C.11)

The term
\mathbf{R}_{3}
:

Following similarly as the previous step, we have that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{3}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\|\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}.

(C.12)

The term
\mathbf{R}_{4}
:

By
Lemma

C.7
, we have

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{4}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U})\|\||\Lambda|^{-1/2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda^{1/2}}\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{K\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|}\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\sqrt{K}\theta_{\max}}{\|\theta\|}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\theta_{i}^{1/2}K^{2}\theta_{\max}^{3/2}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}.

(C.13)

The term
\mathbf{R}_{5}
:

By
Lemma

C.5
and
Lemma

C.4
, we have that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{5}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}|\Lambda|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}-|\widehat{\Lambda}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|\frac{K^{2}}{\lambda^{3/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}\frac{K^{2}}{\lambda^{3/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda}\bigg).

By
Lemma

C.6
, we have that
\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}
as long as
\lambda\gtrsim\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}
, which is true by
2
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{5}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}\frac{K^{2}}{\lambda^{3/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\frac{\sqrt{K}\theta_{\max}}{\|\theta\|}\frac{K^{2}}{\lambda^{3/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\frac{\sqrt{K}\theta_{\max}}{\|\theta\|}\frac{K^{7/2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{3}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\theta_{i}^{1/2}\sqrt{\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\theta_{\max}K^{4}}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg).

(C.14)

The term
\mathbf{R}_{6}
:

Similarly to the previous term, we obtain

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{6}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|}{\lambda^{1/2}}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{W}_{*}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}}{\lambda^{1/2}}\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\sqrt{K}\theta_{\max}}{\lambda^{1/2}\|\theta\|}\frac{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\theta_{i}^{1/2}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}K^{3}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}

(C.15)

Putting it together:

By (
C.10
), (
C.11
), (
C.12
), (
C.13
), (
C.14
), and (
C.15
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\frac{K^{3/2}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{3}\theta_{i}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{3}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}};

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{4}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}^{3/2}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}};

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{5}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\theta_{i}^{1/2}\sqrt{\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\theta_{\max}K^{4}}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg);

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{6}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{3}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}.

We now group these terms for simplicity. First, observe that the bound for
\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\|
is no more than the bound for
\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{2}\|
since
\lambda_{\min}<1
and
K\geq 2
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{1}\|+\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{2}\|+\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{3}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{K^{7/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}+\frac{K^{4}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}}+{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)

We now simplify the remaining terms; i.e., the terms
\mathbf{R}_{4}
through
\mathbf{R}_{6}
. We observe that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{4}\|

\displaystyle+\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{5}\|+\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}_{6}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}^{3/2}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{4}\sqrt{\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\theta_{\max}}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg(\sqrt{K\log(n)}+\frac{K\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{3}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}^{3/2}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{9/2}\sqrt{\|\theta\|_{1}}\theta_{\max}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}

\displaystyle\quad+\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{5}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}+\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{3}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}^{3/2}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{9/2}\sqrt{\|\theta\|_{1}}\theta_{\max}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\theta_{i}^{1/2}\frac{K^{5}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}

\displaystyle\lesssim(\theta_{i}\theta_{\max})^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\frac{K^{9/2}\sqrt{\|\theta\|_{1}\theta_{\max}}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\frac{K^{5}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\frac{K^{9/2}\sqrt{\|\theta\|_{1}\theta_{\max}}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\frac{K^{5}\|\theta\|_{1}^{3/2}\theta_{\max}^{3/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{5/2}\|\theta\|^{6}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{9/2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg),

where we have used the fact that
\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{2}\gtrsim K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}
and
\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\gtrsim\log(n)
, the first of which we verified at the beginning of this proof and the second by
2
. Putting these together, we arrive at

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{K^{7/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}+\frac{K^{4}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}}+{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{9/2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\frac{K^{7/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}+\theta_{i}{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{9/2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg)

Consequently, we see that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-18})
, each row
i
of
\mathbf{\widehat{X}}
satisfies

\displaystyle e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{\widehat{X}}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}-\mathbf{X})

\displaystyle=e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})\mathbf{U}|\Lambda|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}+e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R},

where
\mathbf{R}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\frac{K^{7/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}+\theta_{i}{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{9/2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{9/2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg)

(C.16)

In what follows, denote

\displaystyle\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}:

\displaystyle={\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{9/2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}}\bigg),

(C.17)

so that
\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\|\lesssim\theta_{i}\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}.

Step 2: First Order Approximation of
\mathbf{\widehat{Y}}
:

Now, we note that

\displaystyle e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})\mathbf{U}|\Lambda|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})(\mathbf{U}|\Lambda|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q})_{j\cdot}

is a sum of
n
independent random matrices. Bernstein’s inequality shows that this is less than or equal to

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda^{1/2}}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|}\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\|\theta\|}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\theta_{\max}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\asymp\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{i}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg[\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\bigg].

Consequently, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{X}}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}-e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{i}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg[\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\bigg]+\theta_{i}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}

\displaystyle=\theta_{i}\ \Bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{i}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg[\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg]+\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{64}\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|,

since
\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|\gtrsim\theta_{i}
, as long as
\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}\lesssim 1
and that

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|^{2}}\lesssim 1,

(C.18)

both of which are guaranteed
2
, which we will verify now. First, a direct comparison of
\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}
with
2
shows that
\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}\leq\frac{\bar{\lambda}}{C\sqrt{K}}
, which is strictly less than one. In addition, by squaring (
C.18
), we see that we require that

\displaystyle\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{4}}\lesssim 1,

but this is of smaller order than the first term in
\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}
.
Consequently, we are free to apply Taylor’s Theorem to the function
x\mapsto x/\|x\|
in a neighborhood of at most constant radius of
\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}
not containing zero to obtain

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{\widehat{Y}}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}\big)_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{Y}_{i\cdot}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\mathbf{\widehat{X}}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}\big)_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{\widehat{X}}_{i\cdot}\|}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\big((\mathbf{\widehat{X}}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top})_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\big)+\big(\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}\big)_{i\cdot},

where

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{2}\max_{|\alpha|=2}\sup_{\|c-\mathbf{X}_{i}\|\leq r}\|\mathbf{D}^{\alpha}(c)\|,

where
\mathbf{D}^{\alpha}
denotes the partial derivatives of the function
x\mapsto\frac{x}{\|x\|}
, and
r
satisfies

\displaystyle r\leq C\theta_{i}\Bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{i}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg[\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg]+\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}\Bigg\},

(C.19)

for some constant
C>0
. We also have used the notation

\displaystyle\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}\bigg(\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}\bigg),

which is the Jacobian of the mapping
x\mapsto\frac{x}{\|x\|}
. Expanding further, we have that

\displaystyle\big(\mathbf{\widehat{Y}}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}\big)_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{Y}_{i\cdot}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}\bigg(\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}\bigg)\big((\mathbf{\widehat{X}}\mathbf{W}_{*})_{i\cdot}-\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\big)+\big(\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}\big)_{i\cdot}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}\bigg(\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}\bigg)\bigg(\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\mathbf{U}|\Lambda|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}\bigg)_{i\cdot}+\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}\bigg(\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}\bigg)\big(\mathbf{R}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big(\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}\big)_{i\cdot}.

This justifies the linear part of the expansion, where we define

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}\big)_{i\cdot}:

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}\bigg(\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}\bigg)\big(\mathbf{R}\big)_{i\cdot}+\big(\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}\big)_{i\cdot}.

Therefore, it remains to bound this residual. Recall that we already have the bound

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{R}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\theta_{i}\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-18})
by (
C.17
). Consequently, with this same probability, we note that
\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|\gtrsim\theta_{i}
, so that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}\bigg(\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}\bigg)\big(\mathbf{R}\big)_{i\cdot}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\theta_{i}}\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|^{2}}\bigg)\big(\mathbf{R}\big)_{i\cdot}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha_{\mathbf{R}},

since the term
\mathbf{I}-\frac{\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{i\cdot}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}
is a projection matrix. We therefore need only bound the term
e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}
which satisfies

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim r^{2}\max_{|\alpha|=2}\sup_{\|c-\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|}\|\mathbf{D}^{\alpha}(c)\|.

We now note that the mixed partials of the mapping
x\mapsto\frac{x}{\|x\|}
are given by

\displaystyle\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial x_{i}\partial x_{j}}\frac{x_{k}}{\|x\|}

\displaystyle=\frac{3x_{i}x_{j}x_{k}}{\|x\|^{5}}-\frac{\delta_{ik}x_{j}+\delta_{ij}x_{k}+\delta_{jk}x_{i}}{\|x\|^{3}}.

We evaluate this in a neighborhood of
\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}
of radius at most
r
where
r
satisfies the inequality in (
C.19
).
It is straightforward to observe that since
r\lesssim\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|
, we have

\displaystyle\max_{|\alpha|=2}\sup_{\|c-\mathbf{X}_{i}\|\leq r}\|\mathbf{D}^{\alpha}(c)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|^{2}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\tilde{R}}_{Y}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r^{2}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\theta_{i}^{2}}{\|\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot}\|^{2}}\Bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{i}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg[\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg]+\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}\Bigg\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigg\{\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{i}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg[\frac{K\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\|\theta\|^{2}}\bigg]+\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}\Bigg\}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{i}}\bigg)\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\alpha_{\mathbf{R}},

which holds as long as
C
in
2
is larger than t he universal constants above, and hence both terms will be smaller than one.
Therefore, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{4}}+\alpha_{\mathbf{R}}

\displaystyle\asymp\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{4}}+{\sqrt{K}}\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\bigg(\frac{K^{9/2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}{\|\theta\|^{4}\lambda_{\min}^{3/2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{2}\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}{\lambda_{\min}\|\theta\|^{4}}\bigg(\log(n)\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}+\frac{{\sqrt{K}}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{K^{5/2}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\bigg)

which holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-18})
. This is the advertised bound, which completes the proof. ∎

C.3
Proofs of Lemmas
C.5
and
C.7

To prove these lemmas we require leave-one-out sequences, similar to
Abbe et al. 2020
. First we state the following lemma concerning the leave-one-out sequences. The proof is deferred to
Section

C.3.1
.

Lemma C.8

(Good properties of Leave-one-out sequences)
.

Let
\mathbf{A}^{(l,-i)}
denote the matrix
\mathbf{A}^{(l)}
with its
i
’th row and column replaced with
\mathbf{P}^{(l)}
. Let
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}
denote the leading
K
eigenvectors of
\mathbf{A}^{(l,-i)}
. Suppose that
\lambda\gtrsim\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}
and
\min_{i}\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\gtrsim\log(n)
. Then the following hold with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
:

\displaystyle|\lambda_{K}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})-\lambda_{K+1}(\mathbf{A}^{(l,-i)})|

\displaystyle\gtrsim\lambda^{(l)};

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}.

We now prove
Lemma

C.5
. The statement is repeated for convenience.

See
C.5

Proof of
Lemma

C.5
.

First, let
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}
denote the eigenvectors of
\mathbf{A}^{(l)}
with the
i
’th row and column replaced with the corresponding row and column of
\mathbf{P}^{(l)}
. Observe that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{A})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|

\displaystyle=\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{A})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|+\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|+\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\|\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|+\|(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\|\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}+\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty},

where the final inequality holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
by
Lemma

C.8
and
Lemma

C.3
. Consequently, since
\lambda\gtrsim\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{A})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
which completes the proof.
∎

We now restate
Lemma

C.7
for convenience.

See
C.7

Proof.

First we will argue that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{A})(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}

(C.20)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
. Provided this is true, by
Lemma

C.6
, we have that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty};

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty},

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-19})
. Plugging these in yields

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{A})(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty},

which is the desired bound. Therefore, it remains to prove the claim (
C.20
).

Proceeding similarly to the proof of
Lemma

C.5
,

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{A})(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U})\|+\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\|\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|+\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)\|.

First, we note that the matrix
\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)
is independent from the
i
’th row of
\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}
. The matrix Bernstein inequality (Corollary 3.3 of
Chen et al. 2021b
) shows that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{42v\log(n)}+\frac{42}{3}w\log(n)

with probability at least
1-2n^{-20}
, where we have defined

\displaystyle v:

\displaystyle=\max\bigg\{\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)_{j\cdot}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)_{j\cdot}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})\bigg\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)_{j\cdot}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})^{2}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)_{j\cdot}\bigg]\bigg\|\bigg\};

\displaystyle w:

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq j\leq n}\|(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)_{j\cdot}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}.

For the term
v
, we recognize that
\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij}
is a scalar, yielding

\displaystyle v

\displaystyle\leq\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}

(for details on this calculation, see the proof of
Lemma

C.8
). Consequently,

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{42v\log(n)}+\frac{42}{3}w\log(n)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty},

as long as
\min_{i}\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\gtrsim\log(n)
. Moreover, a straightforward Bernstein inequality argument shows that
\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\|\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}
with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
. Consequently, by
Lemma

C.8
and
Lemma

C.3
, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
it holds that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{A})(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U})\|

\displaystyle\leq\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\|\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\quad+\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\big)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\bigg(\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}+\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\mathbf{U}-\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}.

∎

C.3.1
Proof of Lemma
C.8

We restate
Lemma

C.8
for convenience.

See
C.8

Proof of
Lemma

C.8
.

First, by
Lemma

C.3
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\lambda/\sqrt{\log(n)}.

Therefore, Weyl’s inequality shows that

\displaystyle|\lambda_{K}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})|

\displaystyle\geq|\lambda_{K}|-\|\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\geq\lambda-\lambda/\sqrt{\log(n)}

\displaystyle\geq\lambda/2\gtrsim\lambda,

and that
|\lambda_{K+1}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})|\leq\|\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\|\leq|\lambda_{K}|/\sqrt{\log(n)}
. Therefore,
|\lambda_{K}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})|-|\lambda_{K+1}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})|\gtrsim\lambda
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\|.

Observe that
\mathbf{A}^{(l)}=\mathbf{A}^{(l,-i)}+e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)+\big(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}-e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}
. Consequently, by Weyl’s inequality,

\displaystyle|\lambda_{K}(\mathbf{A}^{(l,-i)})-\lambda_{K+1}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})|

\displaystyle\geq|\lambda_{K}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})|-|\lambda_{K+1}(\mathbf{A}^{(l)})|

\displaystyle-\bigg\|e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)+\big(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}-e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\gtrsim|\lambda_{K}|.

This proves the first assertion. As a byproduct, we are free to apply the Davis-Kahan Theorem to
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}
and
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}
to observe that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|+\|\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)e_{i}e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|}{\lambda}+\frac{\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\|\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|}{\lambda}.

Consequently, we need only bound the numerators above; however, a bound on the first term will also prove the second assertion of this lemma. Note that

\displaystyle e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{n}\big(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot}.

Since
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}
is independent from the
i
’th row of
\mathbf{A}_{ij}
, this is a sum of
n
independent random matrices condition on
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}
. Therefore, the matrix Bernstein inequality (Corollary 3.3 of
Chen et al. 2021b
) reveals that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{42v\log(n)}+\frac{42}{3}w\log(n)

with probability at least
1-2n^{-20}
. Here we note that

\displaystyle v:

\displaystyle=\max\bigg\{\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot})^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})\big]\bigg\|,\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\big[(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot})^{\top}(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})^{2}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot}\big]\bigg\|\bigg\};

\displaystyle w:

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq j\leq n}\|(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty},

where the expectation in the first term is conditional on
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}
.
Observing that
\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij}
is a scalar reveals that

\displaystyle v

\displaystyle\leq\max\bigg\{\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot})^{\top}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})^{2}\bigg\|,\bigg\|\sum_{j=1}^{n}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot})^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{A}_{ij}-\mathbf{P}_{ij})^{2}\bigg\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{n}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}_{j\cdot}\|^{2}\theta_{i}\theta_{j}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}.

Therefore, it holds that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{42v\log(n)}+\frac{42}{3}L\log(n)

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{42\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty}+\frac{42}{3}\log(n)\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty},

which holds as long as
\min_{i}\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\gtrsim\log(n)
. Moreover, we have that
\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})\|\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}
by a direct application of matrix Bernstein again. Consequently, applying these bounds yields that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|}{\lambda}+\frac{\|e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P}\big)\|\|e_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|}{\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty}.

As a byproduct, we also have that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty}+\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty},

which holds as long as
\lambda\gtrsim\sqrt{\theta_{\max}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}
. Consequently, by rearranging, we have that
\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|_{2,\infty}\lesssim\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}
which yields the inequality

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}}{\lambda}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty},

which holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
. Moreover, with this same probability, we have that

\displaystyle\|e_{i}^{\top}(\mathbf{A}-\mathbf{P})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}^{(-i)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\theta_{i}\|\theta\|_{1}\log(n)}\|\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|_{2,\infty}.

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Proof of Second Stage
\sin\mathbf{\Theta}
Bound (Theorem
A.2
)

First we will restate
Theorem

A.2
.

See
A.2

In what follows we give a high-level overview of the proof. Define the matrix
\mathcal{Y}:=[\mathbf{Y}^{(1)},\cdots,\mathbf{Y}^{(L)}]\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times LK}
, and let
\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}
be defined similarly. Since we consider the singular vectors of
\mathcal{Y}
and
\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}
, we will examine the
eigenvectors
of their associated
n\times n
Gram matrices, or the matrices
\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top}
and
\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}
respectively. Therefore, we will view
\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}
as a perturbation of matrix
\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top}
. We expand via

\displaystyle\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top}-\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}+\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}},

where we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E}):

\displaystyle=\big[\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(1)}),\cdots,\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(L)})\big],

where we have defined
\mathbf{E}^{(l)}
as the mean-zero random matrix
\mathbf{E}^{(l)}:=\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}
. Hence,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}+\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}.

By virtue of the tight characterization for each
\mathcal{R}^{(l)}_{Y}
in
Theorem

A.1
, we can see that
\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top}
is
nearly
a linear perturbation of
\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}
. The proof of
Theorem

A.2
makes this rigorous.

D.1
Preliminary Lemmas: Spectral Norm Concentration Bounds

Throughout this section we use the notation
\mathbf{E}^{(l)}:=\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)}
. The following lemma bounds several terms involving
\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})
in spectral norm.

Lemma D.1

(Linear Term Spectral Norm Concentration)
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2};

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim Kn\sqrt{L\log(n)}\Bigg[\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\Bigg]^{1/2}.

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-15}).

Proof of
Lemma

D.1
.

We recall that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})_{i\cdot}

\displaystyle=\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg((\mathbf{A}^{(l)}-\mathbf{P}^{(l)})\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\bigg)_{i\cdot}.

Therefore, we can write this matrix via

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)+\sum_{j<i}\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{j\cdot})\bigg),

both of which are a sum of independent random matrices. Without loss of generality we bound the first term; the second is similar. We will apply the matrix Bernstein inequality (
Chen et al. 2021b
, Corollary 3.3). We need to bound:

\displaystyle v:

\displaystyle=\max\bigg\{\bigg\|\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij})^{2}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)^{\top}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)^{\top}\bigg\|,

\displaystyle\qquad\bigg\|\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij})^{2}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)^{\top}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg\|\bigg\};

\displaystyle w:

\displaystyle=\max_{i,j}\|\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)\|.

For
v
, we note that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle(\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij})^{2}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)^{\top}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbb{E}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij})^{2}\bigg\|\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)^{\top}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i\leq j}\theta_{i}^{(l)}\theta_{j}^{(l)}\|e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{(l)}\|^{2}\||\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\sum_{i\leq j}\theta_{i}^{(l)}\theta_{j}^{(l)}\|e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{(l)}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\sum_{i\leq j}\theta_{i}^{(l)}\theta_{j}^{(l)}\frac{(\theta_{j}^{(l)})^{2}K}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\frac{1}{(\theta_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{4}}\sum_{i\leq j}\frac{\theta_{j}^{(l)}}{\theta_{i}^{(l)}}(\theta_{j}^{(l)})^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K^{2}n}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{4}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\sum_{j}\theta_{j}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K^{2}n}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{4}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K^{2}n}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg).

The other term satisfies the same upper bound. In addition,

\displaystyle w

\displaystyle=\max_{i,j}\|\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathbf{U}^{(l)}\|_{2,\infty}\||\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\|\max_{i}\|\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K}{\|\theta\|^{2}\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{(\theta_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}}\bigg).

Therefore, by the Matrix Bernstein inequality, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{v\log(n)}+w\log(n)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}+\frac{K\log(n)}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\sqrt{\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\max\bigg\{\sqrt{n},\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|}\bigg\}.

Finally, we note that by
2
, it holds that
\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\lesssim\sqrt{n}
, which implies that
\sqrt{n}
is the maximum of the term above. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2},

which completes the proof of the first statement.

For the next statement, we proceed similarly, only now streamlining the analysis. Representing the sum similarly, we have that

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\bigg(\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l}\bigg(\sum_{j<i}\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{j\cdot})\bigg)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}.

We focus again on the first term. Since it holds that
\|\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\|\leq\|\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\|_{F}=\sqrt{n}
, we have that

\displaystyle v

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l}\frac{K^{2}n^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=K^{2}n^{2}\sum_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}

and

\displaystyle w

\displaystyle=\max_{i,j,m}\|\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\bigg)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{l}\frac{K\sqrt{n}}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg).

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-15})
,

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim Kn\sqrt{\log(n)}\Bigg[\sum_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\Bigg]^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad+K\sqrt{n}\log(n)\max_{l}\frac{1}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg).

Finally, we note that as long as
\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\lesssim\sqrt{n}
, the first term dominates. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim Kn\sqrt{L\log(n)}\Bigg[\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\Bigg]^{1/2}.

∎

Next, we bound residual term
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}
in spectral norm.

Lemma D.2

(Residual Term Spectral Concentration)
.

The residual term
\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+nL\alpha_{\max}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-15})
.

Proof of
Lemma

D.2
.

Recall that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=(I)+(II)+(III)+(IV),

where

\displaystyle(I):

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top};

\displaystyle(II):

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top}+(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top};

\displaystyle(III):

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top};

\displaystyle(IV):

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)})^{\top}.

We bound each term separately.

The Term
(I)
:
We note that by
Lemma

D.1
we have the bound

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\left\|\theta^{(l)}\right\|}\left(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\right)^{1/2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim L\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\left\|\theta^{(l)}\right\|}\left(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\right)^{1/2}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle=LK^{2}n\log(n)\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}.

(D.1)

The term
(II):
without loss of generality we consider the first term. By
Lemma

C.1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{n}\alpha^{(l)},

where
\alpha^{(l)}
is the residual bound from
Theorem

A.1
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim L\sqrt{n}\max_{l}\alpha^{(l)}\max_{l}\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|

\displaystyle\asymp KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty},

(D.2)

where we set
\alpha_{\max}:=\max_{l}\alpha^{(l)}
.

The Term
(III)
:
By a similar argument,

\displaystyle(III)

\displaystyle\lesssim nL\max_{l}(\alpha^{(l)})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim nL\alpha_{\max}^{2}.

(D.3)

The term
(IV)
:

Finally, it holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|\|\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim L\sqrt{n}\alpha_{\max}\max_{l}\|\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim Ln\alpha_{\max}.

(D.4)

Putting it all together:

Combining (
D.1
), (
D.2
), (
D.3
), and (
D.4
), we have that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+nL\alpha_{\max}^{2}+nL\alpha_{\max}

\displaystyle\asymp LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+nL\alpha_{\max},

since
\alpha_{\max}<1
by
2
(as shown in the proof of
Theorem

A.1
).
∎

D.2
Proof of Theorem
A.2

Proof of
Theorem

A.2
.

First, by
Lemma

D.1
, we have the bound

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim Kn\sqrt{L\log(n)}\Bigg[\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\Bigg]^{1/2}.

Recall we define

\displaystyle\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big):

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}.

Then the bound can be concisely written as

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim Kn\sqrt{L\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}.

In addition, by
Lemma

D.2
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}

\displaystyle\lesssim LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+nL\alpha_{\max}.

Therefore, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top}-\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim Kn\sqrt{L\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}+LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+nL\alpha_{\max}.

Recall that
\lambda^{2}_{Y}\gtrsim\frac{n}{K}L\bar{\lambda}
by
Lemma

A.2
. Therefore, as long as

\displaystyle nL\bar{\lambda}

\displaystyle\gtrsim K^{2}n\sqrt{L\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}+LK^{3}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+K^{2}Ln\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+nLK\alpha_{\max}

(D.5)

it holds that

\displaystyle\|\sin\mathbf{\Theta}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}},\mathbf{U})\|

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\frac{\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}{\sqrt{L}\bar{\lambda}}+K^{3}\log(n)\frac{\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\quad+K^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\frac{\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}}{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K\alpha_{\max}}{\bar{\lambda}}.

(D.6)

Since the events listed above hold together with probability at least
1-O(Ln^{-15})
, we see that the whole event holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
by the assumption that
L\lesssim n^{5}
.

We now verify (
D.5
). It is sufficient to check that the
\sin\mathbf{\Theta}
bound in (
D.6
) is less than one (which is equivalent to checking (
D.5
)). In fact, we will show that each term is less than (in order)
\frac{1}{K}
, which is the second statement of the result.

2
requires that

\displaystyle C\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{K^{8}\theta_{\max}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|_{1}\log(n)}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{4}(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{2}}\leq\bar{\lambda}.

This immediately implies that
\frac{\alpha_{\max}}{\bar{\lambda}}\lesssim\frac{1}{K}
from the definition of
\alpha_{\max}
.
By plugging in the definition of
\mathrm{SNR}_{l}^{-1}
, we see that we require

\displaystyle C\frac{K^{8}\theta_{\max}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|_{1}\log(n)}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}\mathrm{SNR}_{l}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\lambda}\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}.

Therefore the final three terms being are less than
\frac{1}{K}
since
\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|_{1}}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}
is always larger than one. For the remaining term, we observe that by averaging the above equation over
L
, we require that

\displaystyle C\frac{K^{8}\log(n)}{L}\sum_{l}\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|_{1}}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}\mathrm{SNR}_{l}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\bar{\lambda}^{2}.

(D.7)

By squaring the first term, we see that we need the first term to satisfy

\displaystyle\frac{K^{2}\log(n)}{L^{2}\bar{\lambda}}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{K^{2}}.

This is weaker than the condition (
D.7
).
The proof is now complete.
∎

Appendix E
Proof of Second Stage Asymptotic Expansion (Theorem
A.3
)

First we will restate
Theorem

A.3
.

See
A.3

To prove
Theorem

A.3
we first state and prove several
\|\cdot\|_{2,\infty}
concentration results for the residual terms that arise in the asymptotic expansion, and we prove
Theorem

A.3
in
Section

E.2
.

E.1
Preliminary Lemmas:
\ell_{2,\infty}
Residual Concentration Bounds

The following lemma bounds each of these residual terms in
\|\cdot\|_{2,\infty}.

Lemma E.1

(Second Stage Residual Bounds)
.

The following bounds hold with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
:

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{3}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{n\sqrt{L}\bar{\lambda}}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2};

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{3/2}\alpha_{\max}}{\sqrt{n}\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K^{7/2}\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K^{5/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}}{\sqrt{n\bar{\lambda}}}.

Proof of
Lemma

E.1
.

At the outset, we note that Weyl’s inequality and the condition in
Theorem

A.2
implies that
\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|\lesssim K(\bar{\lambda}nL)^{-1}
with high probability.

We analyze each term separately. First, we observe that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}\|\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\|\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{1}{nL\bar{\lambda}}\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\|.

We now establish a concentration inequality for the term
\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}
. The result is similar to the proof of
Lemma

D.1
, so we postpone it to the end. For now, we simply state that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-20})
,

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{3/2}\sqrt{nL\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}+K^{3/2}\log(n)\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}}

(E.1)

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{3/2}\sqrt{nL\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2},

as long as
\max_{l}\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\lesssim\sqrt{n/\log(n)}
, which holds under
2
. Putting it together, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{3/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{1}{nL\bar{\lambda}}K^{3/2}\sqrt{nL\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{K^{3}\sqrt{\log(n)}}{n\sqrt{L}\bar{\lambda}}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}.

For the next term, we note that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|_{2,\infty}\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|+\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|\|\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim K\frac{\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|_{2,\infty}}{nL\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}L\bar{\lambda}}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|.

(E.2)

By
Lemma

C.2
,
Lemma

D.1
, and
Lemma

C.1
, we have the bounds

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{K\sqrt{\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|};

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\left\|\theta^{(l)}\right\|}\left(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\right)^{1/2};

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}\alpha_{\max}+nL\alpha_{\max};

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\alpha_{\max};

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{n}\alpha_{\max}

Therefore, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim L\max_{l}\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|+L\max_{l}\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{R}_{Y}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\qquad+L\max_{l}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\|+L\max_{l}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\qquad+L\max_{l}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\|+L\max_{l}\|\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\|_{2,\infty}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{Stage\ I}}^{(l)}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim L\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{K\sqrt{\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|}\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\left\|\theta^{(m)}\right\|}\left(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\right)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\qquad+L\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{K\sqrt{\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\sqrt{n}\alpha_{\max}

\displaystyle\qquad+L\alpha_{\max}\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{K\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|}+L\sqrt{n}\alpha_{\max}^{2}+\sqrt{n}L\alpha_{\max}

\displaystyle\asymp L\sqrt{n}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{K^{2}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}+L\sqrt{n}\alpha_{\max},

where we have used the assumption that

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{K\sqrt{\log(n)}}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta\|}\lesssim 1.

(E.3)

We will verify this momentarily.
Plugging this into (
E.2
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim K\frac{\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|_{2,\infty}}{nL\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}L\bar{\lambda}}\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K}{nL\bar{\lambda}}\bigg(L\sqrt{n}\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{K^{2}\log(n)}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}+L\sqrt{n}\alpha_{\max}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{K^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}L\bar{\lambda}}\bigg\{LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}\alpha_{\max}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+nL\alpha_{\max}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{K^{3/2}\alpha_{\max}}{\sqrt{n}\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K^{7/2}\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{\sqrt{n}\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K^{5/2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}}{\sqrt{n\bar{\lambda}}},

since

\displaystyle\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)^{1/2}\frac{1}{(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}\|\theta^{(l)}\|}.

which holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-15})
.

We now verify (
E.3
). By
2
, the definition of
\mathrm{SNR}
, and the fact that
\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|_{1}}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\geq 1
, it holds that
\bar{\lambda}\geq K^{5}\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}
, which in particular implies that
K^{2}\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}\|_{\infty}^{2}\leq 1
since
\bar{\lambda}\leq 1
. This verifies (
E.3
).

Therefore, we will have completed the proof provided we can establish the bound (
E.1
). Observe that

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\bigg[\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbf{E}_{ij}^{(l)}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\bigg]\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\bigg[\sum_{j<i}\mathbf{E}_{ij}^{(l)}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\bigg]\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{j\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbf{E}_{ij}^{(l)}\mathbf{U}^{\top}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l}\sum_{j<i}\mathbf{E}_{ij}^{(l)}\mathbf{U}^{\top}e_{j}e_{i}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{j\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top},

both of which are a sum of independent random matrices. We bound the first term now; the second is similar. We will apply Matrix Bernstein (Corollary 3.3 of
Chen et al. 2021b
). To wit, we need to bound

\displaystyle v:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\sum_{i\leq j}\mathbb{E}\big(\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}\big)^{2}\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|^{2};

\displaystyle w:

\displaystyle=\max_{m,i,j}\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|.

We observe that

\displaystyle v

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l}\sum_{i\leq j}\theta_{i}^{(l)}\theta_{j}^{(l)}\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}e_{i}e_{j}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l}\sum_{i\leq j}\theta_{i}^{(l)}\theta_{j}^{(l)}\|\mathbf{U}\|_{2,\infty}^{2}\|e_{j}^{\top}\big(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\big)(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K}{n}\sum_{l}\sum_{i\leq j}\theta_{i}^{(l)}\theta_{j}^{(l)}\|e_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{U}^{(l)}\|^{2}\||\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\|^{2}\|\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})\|^{2}\|\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{3}\sum_{l}\sum_{i\leq j}\theta_{i}^{(l)}\theta_{j}^{(l)}\frac{(\theta_{j}^{(l)})^{2}}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\frac{1}{(\theta_{i}^{(l)})^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{3}\sum_{l}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{4}}\sum_{i,j}\frac{\theta_{j}^{(l)}}{\theta_{i}^{(l)}}(\theta_{j}^{(l)})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{3}n\sum_{l}\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=K^{3}nL\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big),

where we have implicitly used
Lemma

A.1
. In addition, via similar arguments,

\displaystyle w

\displaystyle\lesssim K^{3/2}\max_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}}{\theta_{\min}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}(\lambda_{\min}^{(l)})^{1/2}}.

Therefore, the result is completed by applying Matrix Bernstein. This completes the proof.
∎

The following result bounds several additional “approximate commutation” terms, analogous to
Lemma

C.4
for Stage 1.

Lemma E.2

(Second Stage Approximate Commutation)
.

The following bounds hold with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10}):

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(K^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\frac{\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}{\sqrt{L}\bar{\lambda}}+K^{3}\log(n)\frac{\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+K^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\frac{\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}}{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K\alpha_{\max}}{\bar{\lambda}}\bigg)^{2};

\displaystyle\|\Sigma^{-2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{3}\sqrt{\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}{nL^{3/2}\bar{\lambda}^{2}}+\frac{K^{4}\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{nL\bar{\lambda}^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{K^{3}\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}}{nL\bar{\lambda}^{2}}+\frac{K^{2}\alpha_{\max}}{nL\bar{\lambda}^{2}},;

\displaystyle\|\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|_{2,\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim K\sqrt{Ln\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2};

Proof.

For the first bound, we observe that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\sin\mathbf{\Theta}(\mathbf{\widehat{U}},\mathbf{U})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(K^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\frac{\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}{\sqrt{L}\bar{\lambda}}+K^{3}\log(n)\frac{\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{\bar{\lambda}}

\displaystyle\qquad+K^{2}\sqrt{\log(n)}\frac{\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}}{\bar{\lambda}}+\frac{K\alpha_{\max}}{\bar{\lambda}}\bigg)^{2},

where the final inequality holds by
Theorem

A.2
, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
.

For the second bound, we observe that

\displaystyle\|\Sigma^{-2}

\displaystyle\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|

\displaystyle=\|\Sigma^{-2}\big(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{2}-\Sigma^{2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{2}}{n^{2}L^{2}\bar{\lambda}^{2}}\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{2}-\Sigma^{2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{2}}{n^{2}L^{2}\bar{\lambda}^{2}}\|\mathbf{U}^{\top}(\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}-\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top})\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{2}}{n^{2}L^{2}\bar{\lambda}^{2}}\bigg\{\|\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\|+\|\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\|\bigg\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{K^{2}}{n^{2}L^{2}\bar{\lambda}^{2}}\bigg\{Kn\sqrt{L\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}+LK^{2}n\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+KLn\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}+nL\alpha_{\max}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{K^{3}\sqrt{\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}}{nL^{3/2}\bar{\lambda}^{2}}+\frac{K^{4}\log(n)\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}^{2}}{nL\bar{\lambda}^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{K^{3}\sqrt{\log(n)}\alpha_{\max}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{\infty}}{nL\bar{\lambda}^{2}}+\frac{K^{2}\alpha_{\max}}{nL\bar{\lambda}^{2}},

which holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
by
Lemma

D.1
and
Lemma

D.2
.

For the third term, we note that we can write the
i
’th row of the matrix in question via

\displaystyle\bigg(\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\bigg)_{i\cdot}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\sum_{j}\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\bigg)_{j\cdot},

which is a sum of independent random matrices. To wit, we bound via the Matrix Bernstein inequality (Corollary 3.3 of
Chen et al. 2021b
). The proof is similar to
Lemma

D.1
(amongst others), so we omit the detailed proof for brevity. Matrix Bernstein then implies that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{l}

\displaystyle\sum_{j}\mathbf{E}^{(l)}_{ij}\bigg(\mathbf{U}^{(l)}|\Lambda^{(l)}|^{-1/2}\mathbf{I}_{p,q}^{(l)}\mathbf{J}(\mathbf{X}_{i\cdot})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\bigg)_{j\cdot}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim K\sqrt{\log(n)}\max_{l}\|(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|\bigg(\sum_{l}\bigg(\frac{\theta_{\max}^{(l)}}{\theta_{\min}^{(l)}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{\min}^{(l)}\|\theta^{(l)}\|^{2}}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim K\sqrt{L\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}\max_{l}\|(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim K\sqrt{Ln\log(n)}\big(\frac{1}{L}\|\mathrm{SNR}^{-1}\|_{2}^{2}\big)^{1/2}.

Taking a union bound over all
n
rows completes the proof of this bound.
∎

E.2
Proof of Theorem
A.3

Proof of
Theorem

A.3
.

First, recall we have the expansion

\displaystyle\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}-\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{L}\mathbf{\widehat{Y}}^{(l)}(\mathbf{\widehat{Y}}^{(l)})^{\top}-(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}+\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}},

where recall we define

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathcal{R}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}^{(l)}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}^{(l)})^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{R}^{(l)}(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}(\mathcal{R}^{(l)})^{\top},

and

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E}):

\displaystyle=\big[\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(1)}),\cdots,\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(L)})\big],

and hence that

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}+\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})^{\top}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}+\mathbf{Y}^{(l)}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})^{\top}.

We now study how well
\mathbf{\widehat{U}}
approximates
\mathbf{U}
in an entrywise sense. We start with the expansion:

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{W}_{*}

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\big(\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{Y}^{\top}-\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}\mathcal{\widehat{Y}}^{\top}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}+\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*})

\displaystyle=(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\big(\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}+\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})^{\top}+\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\big)\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}+\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}

\displaystyle\quad+(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}+(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}+\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}

\displaystyle\quad+(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}+\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*}),

(E.4)

where we have observed that the term

\displaystyle(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{Y}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\equiv 0,

since
\mathcal{Y}
has left singular vectors
\mathbf{U}
. We now expand the first-order term out further. Observe that

\displaystyle\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{*}+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}\big(\mathbf{W}_{*}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\big(\Sigma^{-2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}.

(E.5)

Plugging (
E.5
) into (
E.4
) yields the full expansion

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{W}_{*}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{*}+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}\big(\mathbf{W}_{*}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\big(\Sigma^{-2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}

\displaystyle\quad+(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}+\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*}).

Multiplying through by
\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}
yields

\displaystyle\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}-\mathbf{U}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}\big(\mathbf{W}_{*}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\big(\Sigma^{-2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\big)\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}

\displaystyle\qquad-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+(\mathbf{I}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top})\mathcal{R}_{\mathrm{all}}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}+\mathbf{U}(\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{W}_{*})\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top}

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}+\mathbf{R}_{1}+\mathbf{R}_{2}+\mathbf{R}_{3}+\mathbf{R}_{4}+\mathbf{R}_{5}+\mathbf{R}_{6},

where

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{1}:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\Sigma^{-2}\big(\mathbf{W}_{*}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{2}:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\mathbf{U}\big(\Sigma^{-2}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\big)\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{3}:

\displaystyle=\sum_{l}\mathcal{L}(\mathbf{E}^{(l)})(\mathbf{Y}^{(l)})^{\top}\big(\mathbf{\widehat{U}}-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\big)\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{4}:

\displaystyle=-\mathbf{U}\mathbf{U}^{\top}\mathcal{L}(\mathcal{E})\mathcal{Y}^{\top}\mathbf{\widehat{U}}\widehat{\Sigma}^{-2}\mathbf{W}_{*}^{\top};

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}_{5}:
